Power Pvt. Ltd. and M/s Kleio Solar Power Pvt. Ltd., 220kV S/c line on D/c towers (Route length is approximately | [|ता) 


The above transmission project is already approved by Government of India through the Ministry of Power under Section-68 
() of the Electricity Act, 2003 through letter no. No.25-7/5/2023-PG dated l8th December, 2023.
